  (`E[lnW]=ln(3/4)<0`). **Structural sibling of She–Levêque:** Collatz `β^N` with N~**Geometric** vs SL `β^N` with N~**Poisson**.
  Fits R8's Bernoulli-convolution register — a free structural remark for the qx+1 paper.
- **★ POSITIVE:** **the "3" has two readings that coincide only because `p_v = 2^−v`** — `E[2^−v]=Σ4^−v=1/3` (breakdown

## Active framework synthesis

**The trajectory measure on Z_2** (= the survivor-conditioned residue distribution D_avg(r) for r mod 32) has the following multi-layer characterization:

1. **Integer-level identification** (R58): D_avg = mod-32 marginal of the inverse Collatz tree from m=1 weighted by subtree-size, value-truncated at N=2^22. Pearson 0.86, MAE 0.118 in mean-1 units. Stable across N = 2^16 to 2^22.

2. **3-adic Fourier closure** (R63 → R66 → R70 → R73/R74):
   - |μ̂(1/3)|² has closed form = ½[(a−b)² + (b−c)² + (a−c)²] / (a+b+c)² with (a,b,c) = mass-fractions at residues (0,1,2) mod 3
   - First-principles: (a, b, c) = (1, D+2, 2D−3)/(3D) where D = ⟨inverse-tree path length⟩; → (0, 1/3, 2/3) as D → ∞ (R64.B)
   - At higher 3-adic levels: |μ̂(a/3^k)|² has closed form via Markov chain on (Z/3^k Z)* with v ~ Geom(½) heuristic (R66)
   - Asymptotic decay: average |μ̂|² over primitive a → S_∞ / (2·3^(k-1)) (R66)
   - **S_∞ = 7/15** strongly evidenced (10⁻⁴ extrapolation, R70). Equivalent algebraic identities: S_{k+1} = 3·X_k·⟨ψ−1/3⟩_w (R73) = 3^(k+1)·||d_{k+1}||² (R74). Both give same 7/15 limit via different decompositions.

3. **Structural class** (R59, R62):
   - Multifractal Z_2 measure with wide D_q spectrum (D_0 = 1.00, D_∞ ≈ 0.15)
   - Fourier dimension σ = 0 (atomic-class spectrum, NOT Sullivan-conformal)
   - Resonances at 3-adic rationals reflect (3m+1) arithmetic structure
   - Right literature home: multiplicative number theory measures on Z_p (NOT Bernoulli convolutions or Erdős lacunary)

4. **Mechanism** (R45-R47, R64.B):
   - r ≡ 0 mod 3 are LEAVES in inverse tree (no Syracuse predecessors)
   - r ≡ 2 mod 3 has smaller smallest-pred (≈2m/3) than r ≡ 1 (≈4m/3) → 2× more mass on r ≡ 2
   - Forward Syracuse v-parity → next-residue rule: v even → 1 mod 3, v odd → 2 mod 3 (verified 100% on 1M pairs)

14. **Rate-question reframing 2026-05-05 — within-level vs inter-level operator** ([result_qspectrum.md](result_qspectrum.md), combined with [result_epsilon_6.md](result_epsilon_6.md), [result_epsilon_7.md](result_epsilon_7.md), and [result_77_4_K_spectrum_erratum.md](result_77_4_K_spectrum_erratum.md)):

    **q-spectrum probe across q ∈ {3, 5, 7, 11, 13}** returns universal triviality of K_k^(q) spectrum: |λ_1| = 1 isolated, all sub-leading eigenvalues clustered near zero (10⁻⁶ to 10⁻⁵), no q in the tested family has an eigenvalue near 1/2. q-universality holds at the algebraic level — spectrum structure is {1} ∪ near-zero cluster regardless of prime. q=7 anomaly is **not spectral** (q=7's |λ_2| = 4.5×10⁻⁵ sits in same band as q=5's 5.7×10⁻⁵ and q=3's 2.9×10⁻⁵). (q-3)/q closed form at q ∈ {11, 13, 17} **does not originate in K_k spectrum**; it must live in inter-level renormalization.

    **Combined with k=5,6,7 ε_k results and R77.4 erratum:** the rate-1/2 conjecture as a within-level eigenvalue claim is **universally false**. K_k for any prime q is too rapidly mixing to have a 1/2-rate eigenmode; ε_k's apparent rate-1/2 envelope at k=2..5 (now also walked back by the k=7 non-monotone bounce) was never reflected in K_k's spectrum.

    **The convergence rate question is now reframed:** what is the spectrum of the **inter-level renormalization operator** R_k that maps π_k → π_{k+1} (or its associated dynamic on the residual subspace), and is its dominant non-trivial eigenvalue 1/2? This operator was identified as the right object in R77.4 erratum but its spectrum has not been computed at any q. R77.5 ("inter-level renormalization residual operator") was identified as the natural next probe but never run.

    **What this opens (deferred — flagging only):**
    - Build R_k explicitly at q=3 for k=4..7. Compute its top eigenvalues. Test whether |λ_2(R_k)| ≈ 1/2 (rescuing the rate-1/2 claim at the right operator) or some other value (different rate, different structure).
    - Once R_k's spectrum is in hand at q=3, run the same probe at q ∈ {5, 7, 11, 13} for q-universality at the inter-level layer.
    - The non-monotone ε_k bounce at k=6→7 (|ε_7/ε_6| = 2.36) suggests R_k may have **complex eigenvalues** giving oscillation in the projection; this is the natural test.

    **Publication relevance:** the cleanly q-universal "K_k spectrum is trivial" finding is itself a substantive negative result — settles whether per-level Markov mixing is the rate-controlling object across primes. Pairs with the q-universal q/3 growth ratio (item 10) as two separate q-universal theorems independent of Collatz closure status.

    **Update 2026-05-05 (post-compact, new probes):**
    - **Direct K_k top-10 eigenvalue spectrum at q=3, k=5,6,7** ([result_eigenvalue_spectrum.md](result_eigenvalue_spectrum.md)): |λ_2| = 3.42e-4, 4.41e-4, 1.82e-3 — three orders of magnitude below 1/2 at every tested k. Top 10 magnitudes form a tight cluster within ~16% of |λ_2| (no isolated sub-leading mode). Extends R77.4 erratum to k=7; consistent with the q-universal triviality finding above. Three data points don't constrain k → ∞ behavior.
    - **R̃_k = L · K_{k+1}^m · P inter-level construction at q=3, k=4,5,6,7** ([result_R_operator_spectrum.md](result_R_operator_spectrum.md)): for both lifts (uniform A and conditional-from-stationary B) and both K_{k+1} powers (m=1, 2), R̃_k's leading non-trivial eigenvalue lives at 10⁻⁵ to 10⁻³ — algebraically because the construction reduces to K_k on the level-k space. This R̃ does NOT carry the inter-level rate. The probe's brief targeted ρ ≈ 0.984 (now Tier 5 / falsified at k=11), so the negative result is consistent with that walk-back rather than independent evidence. **Side finding (Tier 4):** under uniform lift, ‖L_A · π_k − π_{k+1}‖_∞ approximately halves per k-step at k=4..7 (ratios 0.4998, 0.4992, 0.5000). Striking 3-point pattern in the same k-regime where rate-1/2 was originally fit and later walked back at k=7..11; treat as candidate finite-k transient until reproduced at k=8..11.
    - **Lagarias / Tao 2-adic density-1 v_2 probe** ([result_density_one_v2_bounds.md](result_density_one_v2_bounds.md)) on `data/v_seq_N8388608.parquet` (2,796,202 odd, coprime-to-3 starts in [3, 8388607]): TEST B (mean v > log_2(3)) returned 100% pass rate, but **the result is mathematically tautological** — direct algebra gives mean_v − log_2(3) = (log_2(n_0) + Σᵢ log_2(1 + 1/(3 n_i)))/L > 0 for every Syracuse trajectory terminating at 1, so 100% on the ensemble simply restates "all 2.8M trajectories reached 1" (already known on this range). **The actual empirical content (Tier 3)** is unconditional ensemble mean v_2 = 2.102 (Geom(1/2) prediction 2.0; 5% deviation, consistent with Tao 2019 measure-theoretic asymptotic at finite resolution). TEST A: per-trajectory geometric null `density(v_i ≥ k) ≥ 2^{-(k-1)}` fails for long trajectories (selection effect — conditioning on large L squeezes mean v toward log_2(3)). Do NOT cite TEST B's 100% pass rate as a quantitative density-1 strengthening of the Lagarias connection — it is an algebraic identity, not new empirical evidence.
    - **Mode amplitudes v2 (2026-05-05)** ([mode_amplitudes_v2_findings.md](probe_mode_amplitudes_v2/mode_amplitudes_v2_findings.md)): δ_k = L_{k-1} π_{k-1} − π_k decomposition onto R_k singular vectors confirms R is involved in slow-rate dynamics but **action is band-collective, not single-direction**. σ_1 captures ≤ 3% of ‖δ_k‖² across k=5,6,7 (2.73%, 3.55%, 0% at k=7 where rank 8 leads). Top-20 capture decreases with k (17.86% → 5.22% → 1.65%) — top-20/dim_R shrinks fast as state space grows. ρ_slow ≈ 0.83 **not present in any single K_k or R_k mode at any k tested**. Top-20 R_k singular values cluster tightly in [0.658, 0.671] across all three k — near-degenerate band, no isolated dominant direction. Decomp A (K_k right eigvec inner-product per brief) captured ≈ 0% — confirmed structural (right eigvec of non-symmetric K is biorthogonal-dual to LEFT eigvec, not to itself; right Perron is constant vector and δ_k has sum 0). **Three remaining hypotheses for ρ_slow origin:** (a) **composition across levels** — slow rate emerges from product of R-actions, not single R eigenvalue; (b) **functional-projection averaging** — ε_k is a specific scalar projection of δ_k that selects the "slow envelope" of the band-collective action; (c) **finite-k recurrence-fit artifact** — true rate near 0.60 (composition asymptotic) and the 0.83 fit is a small-k transient. Distinguishing requires k=8..11 ε measurements and computing R_k composition explicitly.

## Convention note for parallel agents

When adding a new result:
1. **Check this STATE.md** before claiming "rejected" / "superseded" — many results are revisions of older ones with different framings, not contradictions. Especially watch for "apparent inconsistency" between two rates / values — usually it's leading-vs-subleading or different observables of the same quantity.
2. **Result numbers have collided** (R23, R31, R32, R33, R34, R52, R53, R61, R62, R64, R71 all duplicated). When referencing, use suffix .A / .B AND a topic keyword. Eventually renumber chronologically for v3.7.
3. **Update this STATE.md** when adding a result that supersedes an active claim, locks a new closed form, or closes/opens an item in the active path.
4. **Append don't rewrite** the index at top of `closed_form_findings.md` when adding new results — keep existing entries even if the result is later revised; mark status with ↻ or ✗.
5. **Independence is rare.** Almost every cross-result agreement traces back to the single Collatz-map root. Frame "consistent characterization" not "independent confirmation" unless cross-validating against external input (Tao K_h, Chang's exact formulas, classical Cramér-Lundberg, etc.). See `independence_audit.md`.
6. **Honesty over polish.** This archive includes lots of walk-backs (R57 H-dim, R71 1/2 conjecture, Lagarias-Sinai marginal exactness). Documenting walk-backs is part of the rigor signal — don't quietly omit them from new writeups.

## 9 = 3² spine — membership (corrected 2026-07-14)

The "everything terminates on 3²" framing routed the 2026-07 back-half arc. **One of six members was noise and is REMOVED.** The surviving five all fall out of the map's algebra (**4 = 1+3 in Z₃**); the removed one was the only member derived from a *fitted decay rate*, and it is the only one that died. **Lesson: trust spine members that fall out of 4=1+3, not the ones measured.**

**Surviving (algebra-derived):**
1. **7/45 = 7/(3²·5)** — the 9 is squared class-mass (1/3)²:(2/3)² = 1:4 = T_diag (1,4) invariant.
2. **period-9.2** in ε_k — M=18 = 2·3² (class, b_prior) chain, λ = 0.5/|1−0.5·e^{2πi/18}|.
3. **mod-9 darkness onset** — x_{j≥2}=3^{2j−2}·2^{−b} ≡ 0 mod 9 (R3); recast by R82 as 3-adic depth n−2j+1.
4. **R78 support q/9, |F̂|=3√q** — principal-unit Gauss-sum collapse; R81/R81b: the phase is 3-adic-analytic, the Fourier-side view of C1's 4=1+3 wall.
5. **DWM Kraus phase 3^{2j−2}/3^n** on the (1,4) invariant (R82: same chirp species as R78's).

**REMOVED (2026-07-14): inverse-tree single-basin decay 1/9.** Falsified in BOTH measures — R3a (uniform-count: E_MAX-cutoff artifact) and R3a2 (2^{−e}-weighted: well-defined but no clean rate). It was a fitted rate, not algebra. **A future agent must NOT re-add it.**

**Track C (sibling) — what survives the removal:** the *quantitative* rate mechanism is dead, but the *qualitative* paradox is untouched and needs no rate: forward K₋=σK₊σ (conjugate ⇒ S_n identical exact rationals, k=1..4) vs inverse-tree D_n differing 10³–10⁴× (matched-N residual 0.2–4). Statement: **the forward chain cannot distinguish a map with one conjectured cycle (3x+1) from one with three known cycles (3x−1); the inverse tree can** — conjugacy vs non-conjugacy, independent of any decay rate. Standalone-publishable, no Collatz closure needed. The dead *mechanism* (D_n's constant as a clean function of cycle structure) must not take the live *phenomenon* (forward-blind, backward-sighted) with it.

**R81 — F̂ phase profile on the R78 support (2026-07-13). Verdict: H_GROWING_DEGREE (refutes H_QUAD; not fixed-degree, not pseudo-random).**
Mapped arg F̂(3a) on supp={3a: a in a fixed coset mod 3} (|F̂|=3√q const, Th 78.3; smoke PASS) for r∈{2,3,4,5,6} and all 6 c_{ℓ,ε}=2^ε·(1+3^r)^ℓ (ε=0→a≡1, ε=1→a≡2 mod 3). Phase certified EXACTLY: F̂(3a)²/(9q)=σ·ζ_q^s a root of unity in Z[ζ_q] via integer cyclotomic reduction mod Φ_q (σ=−1 for r even = √−3 quadratic-Gauss twist); exact index J₄∈Z/4q. Degree via finite differences in b (a=a0+3b, consecutive → branch-free; sidesteps the singular-mod-3 Vandermonde).

Participation ratio 1/‖π‖²~3^k for every q. So ‖π_k‖²~(1/3)^k (q-indep rate) ⇒ X_k=q^k‖π‖²~(q/3)^k. **Task C:** same factorization gives c̃_q=(q-3)/q (pillar 2), leading const 1, difference→1-3/q. **R64.B — 1:4 class-mass ratio: PROVENANCE CLOSED (2026-07-14). CONFIRMED elementary + exact.** Reconstructs the load-bearing ratio flagged as a gap in the §4 note above. **Class ± = v-parity** (R77 l.14, R66 chain rule: v even→+, v odd→−) — NOT the K_−=σK_+σ symmetry my flag guessed; the real origin is simpler. Two exact routes (Fractions): (a) class probs = Geom(½) parity split, P(v even)=Σ_{even}2^{−v}=**1/3**, P(v odd)=**2/3**; Plancherel is |μ̂|² (quadratic in class amplitude) so P_+:P_−=(1/3)²:(2/3)²=**1:4** = the (1,4) eigenvector. (b) |μ̂|² weight = Σ4^{−v} parity split: Σ_{even}4^{−v}=**1/15**, Σ_{odd}4^{−v}=**4/15** → 1:4 = T_diag prefactors 3·(1/15)=1/5, 3·(4/15)=4/5 → T_diag=(1/5)[[1,1],[4,4]], verified eigenvalues {1,0}/eigenvector (1,4). Numeric on 40M real Collatz v=v₂(3n+1): P(v even)=0.33333, squared-class-mass ratio=**4.0000**. **Gap was write-up only; value always correct → the one genuine provenance hole in D3's unconditional-c=7/45 chain (R75+R76+R77+R64.B) is closed, in the theorem's favor** (R64.B is a one-line Geom(½) parity identity, not a deep input). Self-check bug (double-counted halving weight in an empirical 4^{−v} sub-test → showed 8 not 4) caught + fixed pre-verdict. Files: result_64B.md + result_64B_verify.py + result_64B_log.txt.

**R85 Phase 1 — evidential DWM bridge, design A (2026-07-14). Verdict: VOID — category-invalid design; superseded by rung 1. [Entry added 2026-07-15 in STATE re-audit; the run was undocumented in STATE, though its kill is documented in result_85_bridge.md §Setup.]**
Fired 13:01, six minutes after the Phase 0 memo declared it "READY / NOT fired" (that label was never updated — corrected above). **Validation gate PASSED and is the salvage:** n=3 native vs archived Syracuse targets, G1 +1.07831e-01 / G2_sum +6.08879e-01 / G2_tr +5.35722e-02 / G2_d1 +5.74203e-02 / G2_vac +4.77548e-03, **all ratio 1.0000** — the harness reproduces archived targets exactly and is sound + reusable for any future bridge design. **The evidential result is VOID.** It reported "phase-diff spread≈0 for ε=0 (same chirp) / 1.80 rad for ε=1 (differs)" at n=5/r=2 and n=6/r=3. Both the design and that pattern die: the probe used a **placeholder DWM chirp multiplier (=1)** and compared **pointwise on ⟨4⟩**, but `probe_85_orbit_structure.py` then measured the *real* chirp argument `Δ=2^{−b}(2^{−v}−2^{−v'})` spanning the **whole ring** (34.6% ⟨4⟩ / 34.6% 2⟨4⟩ / 30.8% non-units, identical at n=5,6) ⇒ **2/3 of DWM arguments lie outside F̂'s ⟨4⟩ support**, so a pointwise chirp-vs-F̂ swap is a **category error**. The ε-split was reading the **coset bit** (ε→coset, since ⟨4⟩=⟨2²⟩ is the entire Sylow-3 part and R81's 6 families collapse to 2 chirp-classes), **not the bridge** — and per R82 step-1 the pointwise identity is *forced* anyway, so it carried no evidential content. **Superseded by rung 1** (the valid test is Fourier: operator-DFT at n=6/r=3). **Nothing propagated** — no void result reached a conclusion; the invalidation was caught and disposed of within the same session, before the rung-1 writeup. **Method note for future agents: the failure was designing the comparison before measuring the object's support.** `probe_85_orbit_structure.py` is the cheap measurement that should precede any chirp-comparison design. Files: probe_85_dwm_bridge_evidential.py + result_85_log.txt. Disposition: result_85_bridge.md §"Setup — the placeholder was invalid; measure fixed it".

**BGT candidate E — second-plateau test on ε_2..ε_16 (2026-07-14). Verdict: NO_FIT. The last BGT PARTIAL closes; the Tauberian corpus is now fully disposed. [Entry added 2026-07-15 in STATE re-audit — the probe ran at 21:42, six minutes after the previous STATE write, and was never logged.]**
Ran on **existing ε data — zero new compute.** `BGT_DISPOSITION` l.60 gated this branch on "ε_9..ε_K for K≥15–20", costed at ~tens–hundreds of hours of Markov-chain runs — **but ε_16 has existed since 2026-05-17**, so the escalation branch was affordable for two months and nobody noticed. **Test:** `L(k)=|ε_k|·2^k`; slow variation (a second plateau) ⇔ `L(k+1)/L(k)→1`. **Result — three regimes, no plateau:** regime 1 (k=2..6) L∈[0.0319,0.0407], mean **0.0374** (≈|−1/30|=0.0333) — the original plateau; **k=7 jump** L(7)/L(6)=**4.72** (the load-bearing obstruction); k=9 near-zero sign flip (ε_9=−7.5e−06, + for k≥10, period-9); **regime 3 (k≥10) grows GEOMETRICALLY**, L(k+1)/L(k) = [4.168, 3.029, 2.592, 2.434, 2.320, **2.252**] decreasing toward **1.968 = 2·0.984**, i.e. `L(k)~(2·0.984)^k` because `|ε_k|~0.984^k` (subdominant rate 0.984 > 1/2). **Exponential growth is OUTSIDE the entire regular-variation framework** (RV = power-law / slowly-varying tails), so candidate E closes **PARTIAL → NO_FIT exactly as pre-registered** for the escalation branch. The k=7 jump is **regime-1→regime-3 onset** — the transient (1/2)^k giving way to the true 0.984^k rate — **NOT a plateau-to-plateau step.** No single-regime BGT theorem fits; per BGT_DISPOSITION this would need (a) a multi-singularity Tauberian theorem (candidate I, BLOCKER) or (b) a piecewise-sequential-RV extension not in BO 2020. **Multi-regime obstruction CONFIRMED STRUCTURAL.** **Cross-check byproduct:** `L(k)~1.968^k` and `|ε_k|^{1/k}` (k=13..16: 0.6388→0.6689→0.6939→0.7152) **independently re-confirm the subdominant rate ρ≈0.984** from a route that never fit it — consistent with the data-limited extrapolation in THEOREM_C_745 §4. **Lesson: check whether an existing dataset already clears a cost-gate before treating the gate as live** — this gate was void for two months. THEOREM_C_745 + Th 78.1–78.3 UNTOUCHED. Files: probe_bgt_E_plateau.py + result_bgt_E_plateau_log.txt.

**R6 (qx+1 paper) — does R76 generalize to (Z/q^k)*? (2026-07-15). Verdicts: H_CONS CONFIRMED (verbatim, FREE) / H_EQUAL REFUTED / H_LEAD_BREAKS CONFIRMED. The paper's stated "one line to a theorem" is MISCHARACTERIZED — the R76 route is BLOCKED, not incomplete.**
Scopes R5's single flagged open step ("uniform diagonal-self-overlap domination on (Z/q^k)*, an R76-style conservation identity generalized from q=3"). **Gate PASS** — reproduced result_76_conservation_law.md §3 to 10 digits (M_2(1)=S_2=0.4761904762=10/21; M_2(4)=−0.2380952381; S_2=−2·M_2(4)) before any new claim. **H_CONS CONFIRMED:** `Σ_{j=0}^{q−1} M_{k+1}(η_0+j·q^k)=0` at ≤2.9e−15 for q=3,5,7,11 **and composite q=9,15** — and it is **provable by inspection, no new work**: R76's Thm 76.1 is a complete-character-sum vanishing (`Σ_j e^{2πi rξj/q}`=0 unless q|rξ; `gcd(r,q)=1` ⇒ reduces to q∤ξ). **Nothing in that proof uses q=3** — not primality, not the modulus, only gcd(r,q)=1. **⇒ conservation CANNOT be the missing step: if it were, the paper would be finished — the port costs one line and is done.** **H_EQUAL REFUTED** (the pre-registered hopeful branch that would have saved the port — that 76.3's "−2" is really "−(q−1)"): threshold 1e−10, measured spread **0.97–5.71**; at q=5, predicted S=−0.565 vs true +1.367. **Why q=3 is special — arithmetic, not structure:** Lemma 76.2's pairing **does** port (`(1+j·q^k)(1+(q−j)·q^k)≡1 mod q^{k+1}` ⇒ j↔q−j mutual inverses, M equal on pairs — **directly visible as a palindrome** in measured M at every q≥5: q=5 [a,b,b,a], q=7 [a,b,c,c,b,a], q=11 [a,b,c,d,e,e,d,c,b,a]); exactly one lift (=1) is self-inverse for every odd q. What breaks is the **count**: Thm 76.3 collapses S_{n+1} onto a single mode only because **(q−1)/2 = 1, and (q−1)/2=1 ⟺ q=3**. For q≥5 conservation is 1 equation in (q−1)/2 ≥ 2 unknowns — underdetermined. **⇒ uniform domination needs a genuinely DIFFERENT argument** (direct decay/spectral bound on sub-leading characters), not a conservation collapse. **R5's rate result NOT threatened** — `S_k^(q)~(q/3)^k` stays H_PROVED-at-mechanism; the derivation (1/3=Σ4^{−v}=E[2^{−v}], q-blind) never routed through 76.3; falsifier + pillars 2/3 untouched. **Only the roadmap changes.** **Consolation prize (paper-usable):** a **second, structural sense in which q=3 is critical** — the unique odd q where conservation determines the leading mode, with a one-line proof ((q−1)/2=1 ⟺ q=3). The paper currently justifies "q=3 critical" only via S_k^(3)→7/15; this adds a phase-boundary reason in exactly the register the Bernoulli-convolution template wants. **⚠️ FLAGGED — object-identification gap:** M(1) at q≥5 grows (1.37/4.26/9.80 at q=5/7/11); **R76's S_n (coprime-restricted Plancherel of deviation d_k) and the q-sweep's S_k^(q)=q^k‖π_k‖² (stationary L² mass) share a name and coincide at q=3, but this probe did NOT verify they are the same object off q=3.** Every cross-q claim built on R76 machinery is contingent on it — **pin before building further** (next probe). Not at stake: THEOREM_C_745, Th 78.1–78.3, R81b, ε_k. Files: probe_6_conservation_generalize.py + result_6_conservation_generalize.md + result_6_conservation_log.txt.

**R7 (qx+1 paper) — OBJECT IDENTIFICATION: the q-sweep and R76 have been computing the SAME object by different routes for two months (2026-07-15). H_ID_A REFUTED / H_ID_B CONFIRMED / H_ID_C SPLIT (author prior WRONG) / H_ID_D SPLIT.**
Closes R6's flagged ⚠️ gap. **Tension:** R74/R75/R76's `S_k := q^k‖d_k‖²` (level-incremental deviation, `‖d_k‖²=Σ(π_k(r')−π_{k−1}(par)/q)²`, Plancherel `=Σ_{gcd(ξ,q)=1}|μ̂_k(ξ)|² =: M_k(1)`) vs the q-sweep's `X_k := q^k‖π_k‖²` (raw stationary mass, = probe_5 X_gen). the name collision is real. **H_ID_B CONFIRMED ≤1.8e−15** (q=3,5,7,11): `M_k(1)=q^k‖d_k‖²` ⇒ **R74's identity AND R75's Thm 75.1 Plancherel BOTH port to general q.** **Machinery triage now COMPLETE: R74 ✓ ports, R75 ✓ ports, R76 Thm 76.1 (conservation) ✓ ports, R76 Thm 76.3 (leading-mode collapse) ✗ — and R6 gave the reason ((q−1)/2=1 ⟺ q=3).** **★ DISCOVERED (not pre-registered, spotted in-table then verified AND proved): `X_k − X_{k−1} = M_k(1)` EXACTLY at every q** (≤1.8e−15). **One-line proof** from the projection/consistency property alone: each parent has exactly q coprime children whose masses sum to the parent's, so `Σ_{r'}π_{k−1}(par)²=q‖π_{k−1}‖²` and `Σ_{r'}π_k(r')π_{k−1}(par)=‖π_{k−1}‖²` ⇒ `‖d_k‖²=‖π_k‖²−(1/q)‖π_{k−1}‖²` ⇒ `q^k‖d_k‖²=X_k−X_{k−1}`. ∎ So **`X_k = X_0 + Σ_{j≤k}M_j(1)`** — the q-sweep's object is the CUMULATIVE SUM of R76's object. **★★ UNIFICATION: `c̃_q = D_k/(q/3)^k` with `D_k=X_k−X_{k−1}` ⇒ `c̃_q = M_k(1)/(q/3)^k` — pillar 2 was ALWAYS about R76's object.** Verified against R5's own published numbers: q=7 k=3 → **0.78258 = 0.78258 exact**; q=11 → 0.72881 vs 0.72880; q=5 consistent drift. **Two threads (R74/R75/R76 Collatz-closure since 2026-05-03; qx+1 q-sweep since 2026-05-04) computing one object by two routes.** R76 machinery applies to pillar 2 DIRECTLY; R6's triage is correctly aimed. **H_ID_C SPLIT:** `M_k(1)→7/15` at q=3 CONFIRMED — its deviations reproduce **R76 §10's ε_k exactly** (9.524e−03/5.092e−03/2.452e−03 at k=2,3,4 = ε_2/ε_3/ε_4) ⇒ M_k(1) **is** R76's S_k, checked against published values. **But `X_k(q=3)→1` REFUTED — author prior WRONG:** X_k GROWS (1.667/2.143/2.604/3.069), **linearly**, `X_k≈(7/15)·k`, because at q=3 the increments →7/15≠0 and never decay. **H_ID_D SPLIT (decisive):** `M_k(1)` carries the (q/3)^k rate at **EVERY** q **including critical q=3** (ratio/(q/3)=1.00572/0.99609/1.00110/0.99942 at q=3/5/7/11); `X_k` carries it **only for q≥5**, inherited by geometric summation (Σ(q/3)^j~(q/3)^k·q/(q−3)) — **at q=3 the geometric factor is trivial and X_k degenerates to linear growth** (ratio 1.178 at k=4, →1 only like 1+1/k, polynomially). **⇒ CONSEQUENCE: the paper's primitive is `M_k(1)=q^k‖d_k‖²`, NOT `X_k=q^k‖π_k‖²`** — M_k(1) (1) has the clean rate at every q incl. the critical q=3 where X_k degenerates, (2) IS R76's S_k so R74/R75/R76 import for free, (3) is what c̃_q already measures. **R5's rate result NOT threatened** (survives on X_k: q≥5 by inheritance, q=3 still X_k/X_{k−1}→1=q/3, just polynomially) — but **pillars 1 (rate, on X_k) and 2 (constant, on D_k=M_k(1)) are stated about DIFFERENT objects in one paper, and the degenerate-at-q=3 one carries the headline. Restate both on M_k(1) before publication** — makes it uniform, sharpens rather than degenerates the critical case, imports R74/R75/R76 free. **LEAD (not a result, prior stated to lose):** `c̃_3=7/15=0.4667` while (q−3)/q=0 ⇒ δ_3=0.4667; pillar 3's `0.82/ord_q(2)` with ord_3(2)=2 predicts **0.41 — within 12%** at a case the q≥5 fit never saw. **R8 (qx+1 paper) — π_k IS a q-adic SELF-SIMILAR MEASURE; the domination step is an OVERLAP estimate (2026-07-15). H_ADDR(gate) CONFIRMED at machine zero / H_LB CONFIRMED / H_DOM SPLIT (q≥5 holds, q=3 FAILS) / ⚠️ my H_DOM decision rule was BUGGY.**
**Reframing (derived, then gated):** iterating from any r_0, `r_k = q^k·r_0·2^{−A_k} + Σ_{m=1}^k q^{m−1}2^{−S_m}` — **mod q^k the r_0 term vanishes identically** ⇒ π_k is exactly the law of `Σ_m q^{m−1}2^{−S_m}` (consistent with STATE's K_k lemma: mixes in exactly k steps). So π_k is a **q-adic self-similar measure**, IFS `T_v(x)=(qx+1)/2^v`, weights p_v=2^{−v}/Z, and **every map contracts by exactly 1/q q-adically** (2^v is a unit). By address: **`‖π_k‖² = Σ_a p_a² [DIAG] + Σ_{a≠a',val=val'} p_a p_{a'} [OVERLAPS]`, DIAG = (Σ_v p_v²)^k → (1/3)^k.** **⇒ R5's "3" is 1/Σ_v p_v² = the address measure's own participation ratio; D₂=log3/logq is the CORRELATION DIMENSION** — makes the constant a known species, not a bespoke identity. **"Sub-leading characters don't perturb the rate" restates as "collision mass = O(diagonal)".** **Truncation is EXACT** (2^{−v} mod q^k has period M=ord_{q^k}(2); P(v≡j mod M)=2^{−j}/Z exactly = probe_5/6 weights) **but therefore Σ_v p_v² = (1/3)(1+2^{−M})/(1−2^{−M}) ≠ 1/3 at small M** (13% off at q=5,k=1) — all work uses the exact value. **H_ADDR GATE CONFIRMED at machine zero** (address enumeration vs power iteration: **0.000e+00** at q=3,k=2; ≤1.1e−16 at (3,3),(5,2),(7,2)) — two combinatorially unrelated routes agree ⇒ **reframing VALID**. **H_LB CONFIRMED:** offdiag ≥ 0 always (Cauchy–Schwarz per fiber) ⇒ **C_q ≥ 1 is FORCED ⇒ δ_q > 0 is a THEOREM, not an observation** — explains why all 8 measured δ in result_4 are positive. (Later a kill shot: R9's best linear fit δ=0.82/ord−0.089 predicts δ<0 for ord>9.2, contradicting this.) **★ ratio_1 = 0 EXACTLY — structural:** at k=1 the coding v_1↦2^{−v_1} mod q with v_1∈{1..ord_q(2)} is a **BIJECTION onto ⟨2⟩** ⇒ zero collisions (measured 0.00000e+00 at q=5,7,11,13; 8 more primes ≤3.3e−16 in R9). **This is the seed of the pillar-3 mechanism** — the first collision cannot occur until a full period shift, costing 2^{−ord}. **⚠️ H_DOM RULE FAILURE (recorded, not retro-fixed):** pre-registered "bounded iff max step < 1.5" **cannot distinguish bounded from LINEAR growth** (linear ⇒ steps (k+1)/k → 1, sails through). The automated line printed "BOUNDED" for q=3 and **is wrong**. Actual q=3 ratios k=2..8: 1.013/1.604/2.069/2.534/3.000/3.466/3.932 — **differences CONSTANT at 0.4655 ≈ 7/15**. **Corrected: q≥5 CONFIRMED** (converging: 0.210/0.361/0.00209/0.00068 at q=5/7/11/13, steps → 1.01–1.03); **q=3 REFUTED — domination fails at the critical case by exactly a factor of k.** **Benign — it IS the known critical behaviour** (R7: ‖π_k‖²~(7/15)k·3^{−k}). **Fourth independent sighting of the same phase boundary** (R6's (q−1)/2=1⟺q=3; R7's geometric-series divergence; R7's X_k linear growth; this). **Cross-check:** at q=3 X_k=1+ratio_k ⇒ ratio_4=2.06865 ⇒ X_4=3.06865 vs R7's power-iteration 3.06864623 — different route, same number. **H_ORD monotone-confirmed but functional form WRONG** (ratio/2^{−ord} spans 1.4×; ratio·ord spans 130×) → handed to R9/R10. Not at stake: THEOREM_C_745, Th 78.1–78.3, R81b, ε_k; R5's rate stands. Files: probe_8_selfsimilar_overlap.py + result_8_selfsimilar_overlap.md + result_8_overlap_log.txt.

**R9+R10 (qx+1 paper) — ★★★ PILLAR 3 REWRITTEN: δ_q ≈ 0.82/ord_q(2) REFUTED by 2.55e13×; the law is `δ_q = 2^{1−ord_q(2)}·(q−3)/q` (2026-07-15). R9: H_GATE ✓ / H_BASE ✓ (base 2.017 vs predicted 2) / ⚠️H_EXP INCONCLUSIVE (rule too weak). R10: H_A_DEAD ✓ / H_CONCLUSIVE — B WINS OUTRIGHT.**
**Mechanism (predicted BEFORE fitting, from R8):** ratio_1=0 (bijection onto ⟨2⟩) ⇒ at k=2 a collision needs `2^{−v_2}≡2^{−v'_2} mod q` ⟺ `v'_2=v_2+j·ord_q(2)`, j≥1; pair weight ~2^{−2v_2}·2^{−j·ord}; **the cheapest collision costs a full period shift ⇒ overlap ~2^{−ord_q(2)}**; offdiag sums ORDERED pairs ⇒ each collision counted twice ⇒ **prefactor 2**. `1/ord` has **no mechanism**. **R9 H_GATE ✓** (recomputed all 8 published δ ≤3.8e−4). **R9 H_BASE ✓ — the sharp test:** free 2-param fit `ratio_2=a·c^{−ord}` recovered **c = 2.01704** vs a mechanism that named **2** before fitting. **⚠️R9 H_EXP INCONCLUSIVE — 2nd buggy rule, recorded:** log-space R² 0.99586 (exp) vs 0.98022 (power), Δ=0.0156 < my 0.05 threshold ⇒ inconclusive **and honoured as such**; the rule was bad (ΔR² between two FREE 2-param fits on 8 monotone points over 165× cannot separate them). Note the competing power law has exponent **−4.4985, not −1** — it is not pillar 3's law, just the thing that tied. **Fix applied in R10: pre-commit NUMBERS, not adjectives.** **★R9 H_R2TRAP — methodological finding:** recomputed linear fit `δ = −0.08903 + 0.81886/ord`, **linear R² = 0.94045** — **reproduces result_4's slope 0.82 and R² 0.94 to 3 digits — while the same law is off 121.5× at q=13, 53.4× at q=11, 24.3× at q=73.** Linear R² is dominated by the 2 largest points; the 6 small ones cluster at the origin where 1/ord is also small. **R²=0.94 was real and meaningless.** **★★ And the fitted law CONTRADICTS A THEOREM:** intercept −0.089 ⇒ δ<0 for ord>9.2, but R8's H_LB **proved δ>0**. 1/ord dies both ways (with intercept: violates positivity; through origin: off 121×). **Why the original OOS missed it:** result_4's "OOS" q=31,127,73 have ord **5,7,9 = INTERPOLATION inside** the fitted range 3–12 — it tested the fit, never the functional form. **R10 CONCLUSIVE (1.6 s runtime):** laws fit on OLD primes only; new primes ord **20, 23, 58**, predictions **pre-committed before the run**, no fitting on new primes. **Self-checks both PASS:** 672,400-address combinatorial enumeration vs power iteration at q=41 → **2.776e−17**; exact-rational X_1 vs float → **0.000e+00**. **Results:** q=41(ord20) measured ratio_2=1.9199e−06 → B off **1.22×**, P off 54.1×, A off ~2e4×; q=47(ord23) measured 2.4011e−07 → B **1.22×**, P 230.3×, A ~1e5×; **q=59(ord58) measured −2.5e−16 = ZERO** → **A (predicted 0.01414) REFUTED by 2.55e13×**; **P (predicted 8.6e−07, nine orders above float noise) REFUTED**; B predicted 8.5e−18 = sub-machine-epsilon (asymmetry stated up front in the pre-reg). **★★★ B's miss is 1.22× at BOTH primes, identical to 3 digits — a PURE PREFACTOR, not a form error.** Backing it out of the large-q (uncontaminated) primes: **2.0132 (q=41), 2.0142 (q=47) ⇒ the prefactor is 2** — exactly the ordered-pair double-count. (Fitted a=2.450 was contaminated by small-q primes scattering 2.1–2.8 from O(1/q).) **⇒ `ratio_2 = 2^{1−ord_q(2)}` and `δ_q = 2^{1−ord_q(2)}·(q−3)/q`** (δ=ratio_2·(q−3)/q verified exact: 0.927=38/41, 0.936=44/47). **Checked vs OLD primes that never informed it: q=73 0.1% · q=41 0.7% · q=47 0.8% · q=127 0.8% · q=31 4% · q=11 8% · q=17 12% · q=7 47% · q=13 49% · q=5 84%** — sub-1% at large q, degrading at small q exactly as an O(1/q) correction should. **⇒ PILLARS 2 AND 3 ARE NOT INDEPENDENT — pillar 3 carries pillar 2's (q−3)/q factor.** With R7 (pillars 1↔2 via Pythagoras) and R8 (the "3" = Σ_v p_v²), **all three pillars now flow from ONE self-similar-overlap mechanism.** **STILL OWED (flagged, not claimed):** the prefactor 2 is *matched* with a plausible ordered-pair reason but only the **cheapest** collision was derived — the full collision count is not done; the O(1/q) correction is unexplained. **Both are counts, not fits — both gettable.** Not at stake: THEOREM_C_745, Th 78.1–78.3, R81b, ε_k, R5's rate, R7, R6. **R11 (qx+1 paper) — ★★ THE COLLISION COUNT: pillar 3's leading term is now an EXACT IDENTITY (2026-07-15). H_V1MOD CONFIRMED(exact) / H_FAMA CONFIRMED as EXACT IDENTITY (after 2 errors of mine) / H_DOM_A CONFIRMED(99.3%) / ⚠️H_RESID REFUTED — my prior LOST, and R9/R10's O(1/q) framing is WITHDRAWN.**
**H_V1MOD — the structural fact, EXACT:** `value(v_1,v_2)=2^{−v_2}+q·2^{−(v_1+v_2)} mod q²`; the second term carries a factor q ⇒ needs `A=v_1+v_2` only **mod d=ord_q(2)** (since 2^d≡1 mod q) ⇒ **`v_1 → v_1+d` leaves the value EXACTLY unchanged; v_1 is only ever determined mod d.** Verified by exact integer equality at q=11,13,17,31,41,47 (every row). **⇒ This OVERTURNS R9/R10's story of "the cheapest collision costs a full period shift": collisions are NOT cheap, they are STRUCTURAL and always present — every value-bucket contains a whole GEOMETRIC TOWER in v_1.** **H_FAMA — the count.** Family (a) = same v_2, v_1≡v'_1 mod d. With x=2^{−d}, tM=2^{−M}, M=ord_{q²}(2) (**=d·q at every tested prime, verified**): `G_c=Σ_{v≡c}p_v=2^{−c}/(1−x)` (Z cancels exactly); `H_c=4^{−c}(1+tM)/((1−x²)(1−tM))`; bucket offdiag = `p_{v_2}²(G_c²−H_c)`. Summing `Σ_{c=1}^d 4^{−c}=(1−x²)/3` and `P2=(1/3)(1+tM)/(1−tM)`: **`ratio_(a) = (1+x)/(1−x)·(1−tM)/(1+tM) − 1` [EXACT] → 2x/(1−x) → 2^{1−ord_q(2)}.** **★ THE PREFACTOR 2 IS THE CROSS-TERM OF THE GEOMETRIC TOWER** — `(1+x)/(1−x)−1 = 2x/(1−x)` — **NOT ordered-pair double-counting. R9/R10's stated explanation for the 2 was WRONG** (right number, wrong reason); corrected here. **⚠️TWO ERRORS OF MINE, RECORDED. (1) 3rd buggy decision rule this arc:** pre-registered "H_FAMA ✓ iff |rel err|<1e−9" **fired REFUTED at 1.222e−09** — the rule was mis-specified (a RELATIVE tolerance on a quantity shrinking to 2.4e−7 while float noise stays at machine epsilon; **absolute** errors were 2.9e−16/2.7e−17/1.4e−17 = noise floor). *(Prior failures: "bounded iff step<1.5" couldn't see linear growth (R8); ΔR² couldn't separate free fits (R9).)* **(2) EXACT ARITHMETIC CAUGHT A REAL ALGEBRA ERROR THAT FLOAT HID:** Fraction equality vs my FIRST closed form `2x(1−tM)/((1−x)(1+tM))` returned **False at all 6 primes while floats agreed to 18 SIGNIFICANT DIGITS**; the discrepancy is exactly **−2·2^{−M}/(1+2^{−M})** (≈1e−33 at q=11, ≈1e−247 at q=41), confirmed by exact match at q=11,17,31,41. The corrected form gives **exact Fraction equality at every prime**. Leading term + prefactor 2 unaffected. **METHOD NOTE: run the exact-arithmetic check even when float agrees to 18 digits.** **H_DOM_A CONFIRMED:** family (a) = **99.345% (q=41) / 99.294% (q=47) / 99.323% (q=31)** of total offdiag. **⚠️H_RESID REFUTED — prior LOST:** family (b) (different v_2) was predicted to be the O(1/q) correction shrinking with q. **It does NOT shrink:** resid/family(a) = 0.058(q11) / **0.382(q13)** / 0.097(q17) / 0.0068(q31) / 0.0066(q41) / 0.0071(q47) — positive everywhere (consistent with H_LB), **erratic at small q then FLAT at ~0.7% across q=31→47. A plateau, not a decay.** Mechanically plausible: family (b) is **also O(x)** (pairs cost 2^{−jd}=x^j) ⇒ resid/family(a) ~ const, not ~1/q. **⇒ R9/R10's `(1+O(1/q))` framing is UNSUPPORTED — restate as `(1+ε_q)`, ε_q≈0.007 for q≥31, erratic below.** Honest limit: 3 large-q points cannot distinguish plateau from slow decay; either way my prior does not survive. **PILLAR 3 NOW:** leading term = **THEOREM** (exact identity, exact-rational verified); structural fact (v_1 mod d ⇒ tower ⇒ the 2) = **THEOREM**; **residual (family b) = OPEN** — needs the compensating-v_1 solvability condition `2^{−A'} ≡ 2^{−A} + j·t·2^{−v_2} (mod q)`, solvable iff RHS lands in ⟨2⟩ — **a genuinely arithmetic condition (explains why small q is erratic); that is the next count.** `δ_q = ratio_2·(q−3)/q` unchanged (R10, exact at q=41,47). Not at stake: THEOREM_C_745, Th 78.1–78.3, R81b, ε_k, R5's rate, R6, R7. **R10's LAW stands (measured conclusively); this probe corrects R10's EXPLANATION of the prefactor and withdraws its O(1/q) framing.** Files: probe_11_collision_count.py + result_11_collision_count.md + result_11_collision_count_log.txt.

**R12 (qx+1 paper) — ★★★ TAO 2019 READ: Prop 1.17 does NOT give our result, and the reason NAMES our criticality. `H₁` vs `H₂`, and `D₂ = 1 ⟺ q = 3` (2026-07-15). Literature + framing; no probe.**
Source: `archemedian_eigenvalues/pdfs/arxiv_1909.03562_Tao_Collatz.pdf` (arXiv:1909.03562v5). Read on R11's routing "READ TAO before deriving anything else" — user has ~8 copies locally. **(1) Prop 1.17 does NOT apply — do not cite it as if it did.** Tao: `|E e^{−2πiξ·Syrac(Z/3ⁿ)/3ⁿ}| ≪_A n^{−A}` for 3∤ξ, any fixed A, **uniform in n and ξ** — a **POINTWISE** superpolynomial bound on INDIVIDUAL high-frequency coefficients. **Our object is the ℓ² SUM** `M_n = Σ_{3∤ξ}|μ̂_n(ξ)|² → 7/15`. **Summing Prop 1.17 over the ~2·3^{n−1} admissible frequencies gives ≪ 3ⁿ·n^{−2A} — an upper bound that GROWS like 3ⁿ.** Worthless against a sum converging to a constant. **Prop 1.17 cannot deliver our result at any A.** (By his Remark 1.18 it is equivalent to Prop 1.14's TV/oscillation statement `Osc_{m,n} ≪_A m^{−A}` — still not the ℓ² mass.) His method (§7: 2-D renewal process vs a union of well-separated "triangles", after conditioning on Pascal-distributed pair sums `b_j=a_{2j−1}+a_{2j}`) is built for the pointwise bound; **it is not an overlap count** and does not obviously adapt. **(2) ★ But Tao's Remark 1.15 IS R8's address-counting architecture — with a DIFFERENT ENTROPY.** Verbatim: *"Geom(2) can be computed to have a **Shannon entropy of log 4**; thus by asymptotic equipartition Geom(2)ⁿ is expected to behave like a uniform distribution on **4^{n+o(n)} separate tuples**... the range Z/3ⁿZ only has cardinality 3ⁿ... (in fact this argument predicts a stronger bound of **exp(−cm)**, which we do not attempt to establish here)."* **Tao counts addresses by SHANNON entropy `H₁ = E[v]·log2 = 2log2 = log 4` → 4ⁿ. Our L² object is governed by COLLISION (Rényi-2) entropy `H₂ = −log Σ_v p_v² = −log Σ_v 4^{−v} = −log(1/3) = log 3` → 3ⁿ.** Both exact; `H₂ ≤ H₁` always (Rényi non-increasing in order), gap `log(4/3)`. **THAT GAP IS THE GAP BETWEEN HIS PROBLEM AND OURS:** Tao (TV mixing) has `4ⁿ ≫ 3ⁿ` — a **comfortable surplus** ⇒ mixing holds; **ours (ℓ² mass) has `3ⁿ = 3ⁿ` — EXACTLY SATURATED** ⇒ q=3 is critical, and R8's domination fails there by exactly a factor of k. **(3) ★★ THE FRONT-MATTER STATEMENT (now in the writeup's abstract material):** **`H₂(Geom(1/2)) = −log Σ_{v≥1}4^{−v} = log 3 = log q` EXACTLY at q=3** ⇒ correlation dimension **`D₂ = H₂/log q = log3/log q`** ⇒ **`D₂ = 1 ⟺ q = 3` — Collatz sits exactly at the critical point of its own family.** The `3` is `1/Σ_v p_v² = 1/E[2^{−v}] = 2²−1` — **a property of the HALVING LAW ALONE, multiplier-blind**; the Collatz multiplier merely *happens* to equal it. **That is the entire content of "3 is special," in one line of standard vocabulary**, and it makes the Bernoulli-convolution template (Erdős/Solomyak/Hochman: 1-param family, phase transition located by L² methods) **the same KIND of statement, not an analogy.** **RETROACTIVELY EXPLAINS R85 rung 2:** varying the multiplier 3→p gave `S_k(p)~(p/3)^k` with **the 3 FIXED** (p=3,5,7,11,13) — of course it did; the 3 is the halving law's collision entropy and is multiplier-blind. Rung 2's `7=Φ_p(2)` refutation and its "rate 1/3 independent of multiplier" byproduct are **the same fact seen twice**. **FIFTH independent derivation of the same phase boundary, now the cleanest:** R6 ((q−1)/2=1⟺q=3) · R7 (Σ(q/3)^j diverges iff q≤3) · R7 (X_k linear at q=3) · R8 (domination fails by factor k at q=3) · **R12 (D₂=1⟺q=3)**. **(4) ACTIONS:** cite Tao for the architecture (Remark 1.15) but **state explicitly that Prop 1.17 does not apply** — a reader will otherwise assume it does, and the assumption is wrong by 3ⁿ; **`D₂=1⟺q=3` goes in the ABSTRACT**; `H₁` vs `H₂` is the honest positioning (same object, adjacent question, different Rényi order — not a duplicate, not a competitor); Tao's own unproven `exp(−cm)` remark is the regime he declined to enter and the one our exponential results live in. **STILL TO READ before publishing:** Siegel *(p,q)-adic Analysis and Collatz* (USC 2022/Springer 2024–25, closest sibling) and the **p-adic Bernoulli convolution** literature (**never covered** by the 2026-05-04 dive, which looked only at archimedean BCs + Siegel). Not at stake: R5's rate, R6, R7, R8, R10's law, THEOREM_C_745, Th 78.1–78.3, R81b, ε_k. The open step remains R11's **family-(b) collision count**. H_SPLIT ✓(exact algebra) / ★H_COND ✓ EXACT (0 false-pos AND 0 false-neg, ~450k cross pairs, 6 primes) / H_J1 ✓ at q=41,47, FAILED my threshold at q=31 (4th mis-specified rule) / H_EPS EXPLORATORY — no fit, pre-committed and honored.**

The rule was **d-blind** — q=31 has the smallest d(=5) so x=2^{−5} is largest and |j|=2 carries 11%; a d-dependent threshold was correct. Mechanism intact (|j|=1 dominance is governed by x; total at q=47). *(Prior: step<1.5 vs linear growth (R8); ΔR² vs free fits (R9); relative tolerance vs machine-eps noise (R11).)* **H_EPS — NO FIT, pre-committed and honored** (fitting 6 points is exactly how pillar 3 went wrong). ε_q = 0.0582(q11)/0.3822(q13)/0.0967(q17)/0.0068(q31)/0.0066(q41)/0.0071(q47). **No candidate tracks it: `d/(q−1)` is flat at 0.5 across q=17,41,47 while ε swings 14×; q=17 and q=41 have IDENTICAL (q−1)/d=2 with ε differing 14×.** No smooth function of d or q can do this. **★★ THE ROUTE — and why erratic is the EXPECTED answer:** with H_COND exact, family(b) is a weighted sum over (v_1,v_2,j) of the indicator `1[2^{−A}+j·s·2^{−v_2} ∈ ⟨2⟩]`; expanding **`1[y∈⟨2⟩] = (d/(q−1))·Σ_{χ|⟨2⟩=1} χ(y)`** over the (q−1)/d characters trivial on ⟨2⟩ ⇒ **ε_q IS A CHARACTER SUM.** Same species as Jacobi/Kloosterman — value depends on q's specific arithmetic, not on any smooth function of d or q. **Explains the erraticness STRUCTURALLY rather than excusing it**, and the tool is **standard character-sum machinery with square-root cancellation** — not a fit, not a new theory. Principal character → the "expected" d/(q−1) density; non-principal → the fluctuation making q=17 vs q=41 differ 14× at identical d/(q−1). **PILLAR 3 AFTER R11+R13:** tower structure = THEOREM · leading term = THEOREM (exact-rational verified) · prefactor 2 = DERIVED · δ_q = ratio_2·(q−3)/q exact · **family-(b) collision set = EXACTLY CHARACTERIZED** · **ε_q = OPEN, reduces to a character sum.** Not at stake: R10's law, R11, R5's rate, R6, R7, R12, THEOREM_C_745, Th 78.1–78.3, R81b, ε_k. At k=3 (checked against the chain by hand): **`value(v1,v2,v3) = 2^{−v3} + q·2^{−(v2+v3)} + q²·2^{−(v1+v2+v3)} mod q³`**. The m-th term carries q^{m−1} ⇒ needs 2^{−S_m} only **mod q^{k−m+1}**; and 2^{−S} mod q^j depends on S only mod ord_{q^j}(2)=d·q^{j−1} ⇒ **S_m matters only mod d·q^{k−m}** ⇒ **TRIANGULAR GRADING: v_1 mod d · v_2 mod dq · v_k mod dq^{k−1}=M (full range, no tower).** **RESULTS (exact integer equality, no tolerances):** H_M gate ✓ (ord_{q²}=dq, ord_{q³}=dq² at q=5,7,11,13, all non-Wieferich). **H_G1 ✓ EXACT — R11's k=2 fact survives:** value(v1+d,·,·)==value with **0 mismatches** in 960,000 checks at q=5 and **3,111,696 at q=7 — both EXHAUSTIVE over all M³ triples** — plus full sweeps at q=11 (202,800) and q=13 (290,304). **★H_G2 ✓ EXACT — THE COLLAPSE GRADES:** value(·,v2+dq,·)==value, **0 mismatches**, 800,000 and **2,722,734 exhaustive** + sweeps. **v_2 matters only mod dq.** **H_SHARP ✓ TIGHT both ways, 216/216 at every prime:** v2+d **changes** the value (d genuinely insufficient for v2) and v3+dq **changes** it (dq insufficient for v3) ⇒ the grading is neither loose nor secretly stronger. **H_CELLS ✓** cells = d·(dq)·M = **d³q³** exactly. **⚠️PRE-STATED CAVEAT HOLDS — STRUCTURE, NOT THE RATE:** cells/states = 80 / 31.5 / 1100 / 1872 (= d³·q/(q−1) ≈ d³) ⇒ **the collapse ALONE gives NO injectivity** (flagged in the pre-reg BEFORE running). The weights must do the work (effective 3^k). **Result 1 remains UNPROVED.** General: cells = d^k·q^{k(k−1)/2} vs ~q^k states ⇒ cells/states = d^k·q^{k(k−3)/2}. **★ THE FREE PREDICTION (next probe):** tower ratios are **x_j = 2^{−d·q^{j−1}}: 2^{−d}, 2^{−dq}, 2^{−dq²}, …** — **DOUBLY exponentially separated** (at q=41: 2^{−20}, 2^{−820}, 2^{−33620}). Only v_1..v_{k−1} carry towers; v_k ranges over exactly its modulus. Summing the towers per coordinate (same computation as R11) predicts **`ratio_within(k) = [∏_{j=1}^{k−1}(1+x_j)/(3(1−x_j))]/P2^{k−1} − 1 → ∏_{j=1}^{k−1}(1+x_j)/(1−x_j) − 1 ≈ 2·2^{−d}` for EVERY k≥2** — i.e. **the within-cell overlap is essentially k-INDEPENDENT**, because every tower past the first contributes ~2^{−dq}, which is not small but NONEXISTENT at any precision that exists. At k=2 it reduces to R11's exact identity. **⇒ CONSEQUENCE if true: the within-cell part is BOUNDED in k, so it CANNOT be what breaks domination at q=3 — but domination DOES break there (R8: ratio_k ≈ 0.4655·k, linear) ⇒ the k-growth at q=3 must live ENTIRELY in the CROSS-cell (family-b) part, the same character-sum object R13 isolated.** Sharp, pre-committable, and it is the next probe. PASS — the 7/15 slope uses NO within formula and survives.** **⚠️⚠️ BUT A5 EXPOSED A REAL PUBLISHED ERROR: R15 published "off by 0.06%" against 7/15; the TRUE figure is 0.19%.** The probe computed `off = abs(av − 0.4655)/0.4655` — the deviation from **my own pre-committed PREDICTION** — while the printed label read "vs 7/15=0.46667". Commit 0c5b4db's message is immutable; this entry is the correction of record. **NET: nothing retracted.** All R6–R16 structural claims survive — the power iteration is sound, the grading holds a level beyond where it was tested, the two code paths agree, exact matches float, and the 7/15 slope is independent of the formula it was reported alongside. **One published figure was wrong and is corrected in three places plus the source.** **LESSON (durable): when a probe compares a measurement to a target, the LABEL and the ARITHMETIC must name the SAME constant.** The error lived in a *label*, not in arithmetic — no consistency test would have found it. **R21 — PHASE 2 (literature): ★ our object IS Konyagin's `S(a,G)=Σ e(a·2^k/p)` — right shelf, but the machinery is aimed at the WRONG AXIS (q, not k). This RELOCATES Phase 3 (2026-07-15). No probe.**
Source: `Bourgain-Konyagin/Konyagin_Lectures.pdf` (*Exponential sums over multiplicative groups in fields of prime order*), pulled in the 2026-05-04 dive and **never used until now**. **★OUR OBJECT IS KONYAGIN §1.2 VERBATIM:** "Let g∈Z_p, ord_p(g)=t; S(a,g)=Σ_{k=0}^{t−1} e(a·g^k/p); G=⟨g⟩ ⇒ S(a,g)=S(a,G)." Set g=2, t=ord_p(2)=d, G=⟨2⟩=H — **exactly our subgroup and exactly the index set of our weights w(h)∝2^{−ind(h)}**. Our incidence count (R13/R18: `a+j·s·b∈H`, a,b∈H) is his additive-energy family: **Thm 2.1** N_2(b)=#{x_1+x_2≡b : x_i∈G} ≤ **4|G|^{2/3}** under a size condition; **Thm 2.3** |S(a,G)| ≤ (p·T_k·T_l)^{1/(2kl)}·t^{1−1/k−1/l} via additive energies T_k(G). **⇒ R18's reduction landed on a shelf with 60 years of machinery.** **⚠️BUT WRONG AXIS: (1)** bounds are asymptotic in q and need LARGE subgroups (N_2(b)≤4|G|^{2/3} nontrivial only for large |G|); **our d can be tiny — d=2 at q=3**, the critical case, where every bound is vacuous and H={1,2} is a hand computation. **(2)** NO k anywhere — Konyagin bounds sums at FIXED q; our target is **k-uniformity at fixed q**. **★★CONSEQUENCE — PHASE 3 RELOCATES + SHARPENS:** the k-dependence is NOT in the subgroup sum — it is in the **CASCADE** (R20): a level-k collision needs k successive residue events, per-level conditional rate **≈ 1/q, stabilizing** (L2≈L3≈L4). **DIVISION OF LABOUR:** per-level incidence mass → Konyagin-type equidistribution (his tools, fixed q); k-fold decay → the cascade product (our structure R19/R20, no literature). **REVISED PHASE-3 TARGET: prove the per-level conditional probability is ≤ r_q < 1 UNIFORMLY IN THE LEVEL, with r_q<1 for q≥5.** **⚠️⚠️ CRITICAL CAVEAT FOR THE POST-COMPACTION AGENT:** R20's per-level rate ≈1/q would give c_k~(1/q)^{k−1} at EVERY q INCLUDING q=3 (1/3), which would CONVERGE — but **q=3 DIVERGES** (R15/R16, slope 7/15). **So the per-level rate is NOT the whole story:** c_k = (per-level rate)^{k−1} × (weighted incidence MASS per level), and the mass must GROW at q=3 to cancel the 1/q decay. R16's 3/q (failed as a raw rate) and this 1/q are two DIFFERENT measured quantities; reconciling them is UNFINISHED. **DO NOT assume c_k~(1/q)^{k−1} — it is contradicted at q=3.** Both factors' q- and k-dependence must be pinned before Phase 3 closes. **FOR LATER:** `Bourgain-Konyagin/122 NewExp.pdf` (Bourgain–Chang) covers composite moduli q=p^r + Heilbronn sums (Heath-Brown–Konyagin Stepanov method) — the mod-q^k setting before the F_q reduction; only needed if R20's level-2 F_q reduction proves insufficient. **PLAN:** Phases 0/1/1b DONE (exact) · 1c PARTIAL (level 2 verified 1.86M pairs; levels≥3 not derived) · **Phase 2 DONE (shelf = Konyagin, on disk, wrong axis)** · **Phase 3 RELOCATED: prove per-level rate ≤ r_q<1 uniform in level + reconcile the q=3 divergence caveat.** Not at stake: R10–R20, R5's rate, R6, R7, R12, THEOREM_C_745. Files: result_21_phase2_literature.md.

**R22 — PHASE 3 (reconciliation): ★★ the weighted increment ratio `ρ_k=c_{k+1}/c_k` IS the correct Phase-3 object — →1 at q=3 (divergence), <1 for q≥5 (convergence). The R20/R21 CAVEAT IS RESOLVED. Committed value `3/q` REFUTED (8th quantitative miss) (2026-07-16).**
Probe: `probe_22_phase3_increment_ratio.py`. **OBJECT:** `c_k = cross(k)−cross(k−1)` (R19 new-finest-coordinate mass), `ρ_k = c_{k+1}/c_k`; `cross(k)` via R18 H_P0 stationary route `‖π_k‖²/P2^k − 1 − ratio_within`. **★RECONCILIATION (the flagged post-compaction caveat): DONE.** R20's ~1/q (unweighted per-level collision rate) and `ρ_k` (weighted increment ratio) are **DIFFERENT OBJECTS**; `ρ_k` governs `cross`, and at q=3 it is **1.000 NOT 1/3** — which is EXACTLY why q=3 diverges. `c_k` at q=3 locks on **7/15** (R15 slope) ⇒ `ρ_k→1` (every k≥4 ratio within 0.3%). **⇒ R21's "do not assume c_k~(1/q)^{k−1}" flag RETIRED: `c_k = c_2·∏ρ_j`, `ρ_j→1` at q=3.** **★G2 (phase boundary) CONFIRMED:** ρ: q=3 → 1.003,1.001,0.999,1.001 (→1); q=5 → 0.53,0.51,0.62 (<1); q=7 → 0.45,0.36 (<1); q=11 → 0.18 (<1). The revised Phase-3 target **`prove ρ_k ≤ r_q<1 uniformly in k for q≥5`** is validated as the correct statement. **✗PRED `ρ_q→3/q` REFUTED (8th consecutive quantitative miss):** inconsistent signs — q=5 OVERSHOOTS (0.624 rising past 0.60), q=7/q=11 UNDERSHOOT (0.357 vs 0.429; 0.181 vs 0.273). No clean law. Structural prior held, quantitative lost (arc-invariant). Reconciliation never used the value ⇒ miss costs nothing. **⚠️OPEN + FLAG:** `r_q` for q≥5 NOT pinned (local n·M≤25e6 caps q=5 at k=5). **The q=5 ratio is CLIMBING (0.51→0.62→0.64)** — `ρ<1` at reachable k is consistent with convergence but does NOT secure the uniform bound; a rising seq could approach 1. NOT a probe defect — the uniform-in-k bound is a **SPECTRAL-GAP statement about a FIXED operator**, provable at fixed dimension, NOT closable by finite-k compute. **PLAN:** 0/1/1b DONE · 1c PARTIAL · 2 DONE · **3: object PINNED, caveat RESOLVED, G2 confirmed; uniform r_q<1 is now a clean spectral-gap target — value TBD by PROOF not compute.** Not at stake: R10–R21, R5's rate, R6, R7, R12, THEOREM_C_745. Files: probe_22_phase3_increment_ratio.py + result_22_phase3_increment_ratio.md + result_22_phase3_log.txt.

**R23 — ROUTE 2 (exact-rational increments): ★★ `7/15` is the LIMIT not the exact increment; `c_k` is order ≥2 EVERYWHERE (exact); the q=3 approach is a super-geometric TOWER. Recurrence-hunt under-determined ⇒ route 1 = the RPF TRANSFER OPERATOR (2026-07-16).**
Probe: `probe_23_exact_increment_recurrence.py` (exact big-int arithmetic, no float; cell weight = 2^E/D, per-residue integer numerators). **G_EXACT gate CONFIRMED** (exact cross(k) matches R18 float ≤1.3e-14, 11 cases; reach q=3 k≤5, q=5 k≤4, q=7/11 k≤3). **★FINDING 1 — 7/15 REFUTED as exact:** c_k(q=3) ≠ 7/15 exactly — c_4 off −2.43e-3, c_5 off −1.15e-3, converging to 7/15 from below. **Refines R15 (its slope claim was 2%-level, still holds), does NOT contradict.** Exact form: cross(k)=(7/15)k+const+decaying. **★FINDING 2 — c_k order ≥2 EVERYWHERE (exact, not float):** geometric ruled out by exact rationals c_3/c_2≠c_4/c_3 at q=3 (1.225 vs 0.953) AND q=5 (0.534 vs 0.508). So r_q is a DOMINANT eigenvalue above subdominant modes. **★FINDING 3 — q=3 approach is a super-geometric TOWER not one mode:** second-diff ratios Dc_4/Dc_3=−0.256, Dc_5/Dc_4=−0.056 (alternating, shrinking FASTER than geometric) = fingerprint of the doubly-exp tower x_j=2^{−dq^{j−1}} (R14); corrections die ~2^{−dq^k}. **NO finite linear recurrence.** **FINDING 4 — recurrence under-determined (as pre-reg):** order-2 verify needs 5 c-values; have ≤4. Order NOT fittable from data → route 1 needs STRUCTURE. **★★CONSEQUENCE — route 1 IDENTIFIED:** the operator is the **Ruelle–Perron–Frobenius transfer operator L of the IFS T_v(x)=(qx+1)/2^v**, weights p_v=2^{−v}/Z. Top eigenvalue = R5's D₂=log3/logq; **r_q = SUBDOMINANT eigenvalue λ₂(L)**; RPF spectrum CONVERGES under truncation (unlike k-index) ⇒ λ₂ computable at fixed dim-per-truncation; **r_q<1 ⟺ SPECTRAL GAP of L** = standard provable statement for self-similar measures. **NEXT (route 1): discretize L, watch λ₂(L) converge.** Not at stake: R10–R22 (R15 refined not refuted), R5's rate, R6, R7, R12, THEOREM_C_745. **R24 — ROUTE 1A (transfer operator, no-tower fold): ✗ NEGATIVE but informative — the folded operator is TRIVIAL (total_LO≡0). ★cross overlap is ENTIRELY TOWER-GENERATED; no tower-free finite operator exists. Redirects to A′ (weighted cascade operator) / B (Fourier) (2026-07-16).**
Probe: `probe_24_transfer_operator_A.py`. **Built the "no-tower" fixed operator** (fold every coordinate to modulus d instead of d·q^{j-1}), expecting it to carry the leading rate r_q with the tower as a small correction. **✗REFUTED HARD:** total_LO(k) ≡ 0 at ALL q, ALL k (c_k^LO = float noise ~1e-16). **The folded value-map is INJECTIVE → ZERO off-diagonal collisions.** Hand-checked q=3 k=2: 4 folded cells → values {8,4,2,1}, all distinct. **★STRUCTURAL FINDING:** cross-cell overlap is **ENTIRELY tower-generated** — collisions require exponents differing by j·d·q^{level} (R13 `v'=v+j·d`), which EXIST ONLY because the moduli grow. Fold to mod d ⇒ j=0 only ⇒ nothing collides. **⇒ NO tower-free finite operator; the tower is not a correction to c_k, it is the GENERATOR of c_k** (sharpens R23). **G_TOWER gate:** gap |c_k^LO−c_k^real| = |0−c_k^real| = the whole real signal, GROWING → correctly flagged "folding changed the rate → use B". **★DIAGNOSIS — the CORRECT fixed operator (A′):** keep the shift j as a WEIGHTED transition (weight ~2^{−jd} per extra shift, summable) on state (carry∈F_q, a∈H, b∈H) — R20's cascade but carrying WEIGHTS not counts. State space q·d·d ≤ 1100 (fixed, finite); tower encoded as the geometric j-sum, NOT deleted; gate-able vs R23 exact cross(k). **NEXT (fork): build A′ (weighted cascade operator, directly gate-able) OR B (Fourier/RPF, independent).** Not at stake: R10–R23, R5's rate, R6, R7, R12, THEOREM_C_745. Files: probe_24_transfer_operator_A.py + result_24_transfer_A_log.txt.
